No fsync on commit, three DBMS, cc2 (container sysbench-nvme), 2026-11-04

Durability (same for all): Firebird 5.0.4 SuperServer Forced Writes off + UseFileSystemCache = true
(alias sbwfs in databases.conf), MariaDB 11.8 innodb_flush_log_at_trx_commit = 2,
PostgreSQL 17 synchronous_commit = off. No DBMS calls fsync at commit.
Driver: firebird-sysbench IBSurgeon master 5c026be (sysbench 1.1.0-5c026be), rebuilt in /root/firebird-sysbench.
Method: TCP 127.0.0.1 for all; servers pinned to CPUs 0-2,6-8, sysbench to 3-5,9-11; 4 tables x 1M rows;
golden copy before each write run (FB: file copy of sbw.golden.fdb; MariaDB: transportable tablespaces;
PG: CREATE DATABASE ... TEMPLATE sbgold STRATEGY FILE_COPY) and once before the read tests;
--warmup-time=10 --time=30 (30 s measured); Firebird LINGER 3600, --firebird-stats=on.
2 passes, order per pass: Firebird (client, server alternate per case), MariaDB, PostgreSQL; one DBMS running at a time.
TPS below = mean of the 2 passes; for cases where passes 1 and 2 differ by more than 25 % a pass 3 was run and
the median of 3 is used (marked * in the spread column: spread = closest pair). Cache 1600 MB for every DBMS (FB 102400 x 16K pages).
GC columns: FB OST lag = MON$NEXT_TRANSACTION - MON$OLDEST_SNAPSHOT, max of samples every 5 s of the
measured part (both passes); FB versions = gstat -r total versions of SBTEST1 after the run (max of passes);
MariaDB hist = InnoDB history list length (max sample); PG xmin = max age(backend_xmin) (max sample),
PG dead = n_dead_tup of sbtest1 after the run.

=== Notes and problems
1. Firebird results are valid: OST lag stays small and does not grow in any write run (client <= 214,
   server <= 1,844 at 16 threads). gstat: 0 versions left after update and insert runs, 300-1,300 after
   read_write. oltp_delete leaves 69K-288K versions (back versions of deleted rows that the background
   garbage collector has not reached yet; OST lag is low, so this is GC backlog, not blocked GC).
   MariaDB and PostgreSQL have the same kind of backlog: InnoDB history list up to 333K (delete 16t,
   it grows during the run), PostgreSQL up to 590K dead tuples left for autovacuum.
2. Firebird file system cache: alias sbwfs has UseFileSystemCache = true in databases.conf (as in the
   2026-10-16 runs), firebird.conf was not changed. The driver line shows "forced writes off, file
   system cache on", and the file is open without O_DIRECT (fd flags 02100002).
3. MariaDB cold buffer pool (fixed): the first MariaDB pass 1 (15:10-15:22) ran with a cold buffer
   pool after the tablespace import (InnoDB reads with O_DIRECT; Firebird and PostgreSQL read the fresh
   copy from the OS cache). Stopped, added a preload (full scan of PRIMARY and k_N of each table after
   each restore), MariaDB and PostgreSQL pass 1 run again. The cold results are not used
   (raw_my_cold/ on the stand).
4. MariaDB update_index is unstable with the default 96M redo log: checkpoint age 76 of 81 MB during
   the run, InnoDB flushes pages synchronously, latency max up to 6 s, passes 132 .. 42,854 TPS. The
   main table uses the median of 3. The extra run with innodb_log_file_size = 1G (same size as the
   PostgreSQL max_wal_size) is stable: update_index 10.9K / 29.4K / 58.7K TPS at 1/4/16 threads. For
   update runs the 1G column is the fairer MariaDB number.
5. Host noise: cc2 is shared (VMs use about 21 GB RAM, swap 4 GB full). During PostgreSQL pass 2
   (16:45-16:52) host IO pressure was about 33 % (avg300) and PostgreSQL update_non_index fell to
   779-4,654 TPS. Pass 3 ran again all 13 cases with more than 25 % difference; the median is used.
   PostgreSQL update_non_index 4t is still not reliable (41,994 / 779 / 21,010; pass 3 ran at 45-55 %
   IO pressure): pass 1 (41,994) is probably the real value.
6. Firebird scales worse from 4 to 16 threads in update/insert/delete (for example update_non_index
   client 21.0K -> 14.9K, server 33.2K -> 20.6K), MariaDB and PostgreSQL still grow (6 server CPUs).
7. Each MariaDB tablespace import writes about 37K "[Note] InnoDB: trying to read page ... in
   nonexisting or being-dropped tablespace" lines to the journal (about 1.2M lines in total). Harmless.
8. Left on the stand: MariaDB database sbns, PostgreSQL databases sbgold and sbns, /root/nosync
   (scripts, MariaDB golden tablespaces), /root/firebird-sysbench at 5c026be (the old tree with local
   diagnostic changes is in /root/firebird-sysbench.bak-20261104). sbwfs.fdb holds a golden copy
   (Forced Writes off, as before). Durable settings restored and checked (durable_diff.txt empty).

=== Extra (not the task's configuration): MariaDB flush=2 + innodb_log_file_size = 1G
Reason: with the default 96M redo log the checkpoint age stays at the limit (76 of 81 MB seen during
the main run), InnoDB flushes pages synchronously and update runs stall (latency max up to seconds).
PostgreSQL has max_wal_size = 1GB, so 1G is the same size. Same method, write tests, 2 passes.

=== script lib.sh
# No-fsync three-DBMS run (2026-11-04), container sysbench-nvme on cc2.
# Firebird FW off + file system cache (alias sbwfs), MariaDB innodb_flush_log_at_trx_commit=2,
# PostgreSQL synchronous_commit=off. One DBMS runs at a time.
NS=/root/nosync
SB=/root/firebird-sysbench/src/sysbench
LUA=/root/firebird-sysbench/src/lua
unset LUA_PATH
FBPASS=$(cat /root/fbpass)
FBFS=/var/lib/fbdata/sbwfs.fdb          # alias sbwfs: UseFileSystemCache = true (databases.conf)
FBGOLD=/var/lib/fbdata/sbw.golden.fdb   # 4 x 1M rows, Forced Writes on
ISQL=/opt/firebird/bin/isql
GFIX=/opt/firebird/bin/gfix
GSTAT=/opt/firebird/bin/gstat
MYCNF=/etc/mysql/mariadb.conf.d/99-bench.cnf
PGCNF=/etc/postgresql/17/main/conf.d/bench.conf
MYDATA=/var/lib/mysql
MYGOLD=$NS/my_gold                      # transportable tablespaces of sbns (4 x 1M rows)
SRV_CPUS=0-2,6-8
CLI_CPUS=3-5,9-11
DATA="--tables=4 --table-size=1000000"
FB_ARGS="--db-driver=firebird --firebird-db=127.0.0.1:sbwfs --firebird-user=SYSDBA --firebird-password=$FBPASS --firebird-client=/opt/firebird/lib/libfbclient.so --firebird-stats=on"
MY_ARGS="--db-driver=mysql --mysql-host=127.0.0.1 --mysql-port=3306 --mysql-user=sbtest --mysql-password=sbtest --mysql-db=sbns"
PG_ARGS="--db-driver=pgsql --pgsql-host=127.0.0.1 --pgsql-port=5432 --pgsql-user=sbtest --pgsql-password=sbtest --pgsql-db=sbns"
TESTS="oltp_update_non_index oltp_insert oltp_update_index oltp_read_write oltp_delete oltp_point_select oltp_read_only"
THREADS="1 4 16"
RUNTIME=30
WARMUP=10

log() { echo "$(date '+%F %T') $*" | tee -a $NS/progress.log; }
svc() { case $1 in fb) echo firebird;; my) echo mariadb;; pg) echo postgresql@17-main;; esac; }
psql_() { runuser -u postgres -- psql -X -q -At "$@"; }
fbsql() { taskset -c $CLI_CPUS $ISQL -q -user SYSDBA -password "$FBPASS" "127.0.0.1:sbwfs"; }

# only_engine fb|my|pg: stop the other two, restart this one (loads its config), pin it
only_engine() {
  local x
  for x in fb my pg; do [ $x = $1 ] || systemctl stop $(svc $x); done
  systemctl restart $(svc $1)
  for i in $(seq 60); do
    case $1 in
      fb) echo 'select 1 from rdb$database;' | $ISQL -q -user SYSDBA -password "$FBPASS" employee >/dev/null 2>&1 && break;;
      my) mariadb -e 'select 1' >/dev/null 2>&1 && break;;
      pg) psql_ -c 'select 1' >/dev/null 2>&1 && break;;
    esac
    sleep 1
  done
  pin_servers $1
}

pin_servers() {
  local p
  case $1 in
    fb) taskset -a -p -c $SRV_CPUS $(pidof firebird) >/dev/null;;
    my) taskset -a -p -c $SRV_CPUS $(pidof mariadbd) >/dev/null;;
    pg) for p in $(pgrep -u postgres); do taskset -a -p -c $SRV_CPUS $p >/dev/null 2>&1; done;;
  esac
}

# settings lines (to $NS/settings.txt and the log)
settings() {
  local s
  case $1 in
    fb) s=$(printf '%s\n' \
          "select 'sbwfs: forced_writes=' || mon\$forced_writes || ' page_buffers=' || mon\$page_buffers || ' page_size=' || mon\$page_size from mon\$database;" \
          "select 'sbwfs: ' || trim(rdb\$config_name) || '=' || trim(rdb\$config_value) from rdb\$config where rdb\$config_name in ('UseFileSystemCache','FileSystemCacheThreshold','ServerMode');" \
          "select 'sbwfs: linger=' || coalesce(rdb\$linger, 0) from rdb\$database;" \
          | fbsql 2>&1 | grep 'sbwfs:' | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ';')
        s="Firebird $s $(fb_fdflags)";;
    my) s=$(mariadb -N -e "select concat('MariaDB ', @@version, ': innodb_flush_log_at_trx_commit=', @@innodb_flush_log_at_trx_commit, ', innodb_buffer_pool_size=', @@innodb_buffer_pool_size div 1048576, 'M, innodb_flush_method=', @@innodb_flush_method, ', innodb_log_file_size=', @@innodb_log_file_size div 1048576, 'M, innodb_doublewrite=', @@innodb_doublewrite, ', log_bin=', @@log_bin, ', sync_binlog=', @@sync_binlog)");;
    pg) s="PostgreSQL $(psql_ -c "select string_agg(name || '=' || current_setting(name), ', ' order by name) from pg_settings where name in ('server_version','synchronous_commit','fsync','full_page_writes','shared_buffers','wal_level','max_wal_size','checkpoint_timeout')")";;
  esac
  echo "$(date '+%F %T') [$2] $s" | tee -a $NS/settings.txt >> $NS/progress.log
}

# open flags of sbwfs.fdb in the firebird process (O_DIRECT = 040000)
fb_fdflags() {
  local pid fd fl
  pid=$(pidof firebird)
  for fd in /proc/$pid/fd/*; do
    case "$(readlink $fd)" in
      $FBFS) fl=$(awk '/^flags/{print $2}' /proc/$pid/fdinfo/${fd##*/})
             if [ $(( 8#$fl & 8#040000 )) -ne 0 ]; then echo "fd flags $fl (O_DIRECT)"; else echo "fd flags $fl (no O_DIRECT)"; fi
             return;;
    esac
  done
  echo "fd: sbwfs.fdb not open"
}

fb_open() { ls -l /proc/$(pidof firebird)/fd 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$FBFS"; }
# golden copy -> sbwfs.fdb, Forced Writes off, LINGER 3600
fb_restore() {
  if fb_open; then $GFIX -shut full -force 0 -user SYSDBA -password "$FBPASS" 127.0.0.1:sbwfs; fi
  for i in $(seq 60); do fb_open || break; sleep 1; done
  fb_open && { log "fb_restore: sbwfs.fdb still open"; return 1; }
  cp --sparse=never $FBGOLD $FBFS && chown firebird:firebird $FBFS && chmod 660 $FBFS || return 1
  $GFIX -write async -user SYSDBA -password "$FBPASS" 127.0.0.1:sbwfs || return 1
  echo "ALTER DATABASE SET LINGER TO 3600; COMMIT;" | fbsql
}

# transportable tablespaces of the golden copy -> database sbns
my_restore() {
  local i
  mariadb -e "DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS sbns; CREATE DATABASE sbns" || return 1
  mariadb sbns < $MYGOLD/schema.sql || return 1
  for i in 1 2 3 4; do mariadb sbns -e "ALTER TABLE sbtest$i DISCARD TABLESPACE" || return 1; done
  cp $MYGOLD/sbtest*.ibd $MYGOLD/sbtest*.cfg $MYDATA/sbns/ && chown mysql:mysql $MYDATA/sbns/* || return 1
  for i in 1 2 3 4; do mariadb sbns -e "ALTER TABLE sbtest$i IMPORT TABLESPACE" || return 1; done
  my_preload
}

# InnoDB reads with O_DIRECT, so after the import its buffer pool is cold, while Firebird and
# PostgreSQL read the fresh copy from the OS cache. Read all pages of both indexes into the pool.
my_preload() {
  local i
  for i in 1 2 3 4; do
    mariadb -N sbns -e "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sbtest$i FORCE INDEX (PRIMARY); SELECT COUNT(k) FROM sbtest$i FORCE INDEX (k_$i)" > /dev/null || return 1
  done
}

# database sbgold -> sbns (file copy)
pg_restoredb() {
  psql_ -c "DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS sbns WITH (FORCE)" || return 1
  psql_ -c "CREATE DATABASE sbns OWNER sbtest TEMPLATE sbgold STRATEGY FILE_COPY"
}

# garbage indicator during a write run:
#   fb: MON$NEXT_TRANSACTION - MON$OLDEST_SNAPSHOT
#   my: InnoDB history list length
#   pg: max age(backend_xmin) of the sbns sessions
gc_sample() {
  case $1 in
    fb) echo 'select mon$next_transaction - mon$oldest_snapshot from mon$database;' | fbsql 2>/dev/null | grep -o '[0-9]\+' | tail -1;;
    my) mariadb -N -e "select count from information_schema.innodb_metrics where name='trx_rseg_history_len'";;
    pg) psql_ -c "select coalesce(max(age(backend_xmin)), 0) from pg_stat_activity where datname = 'sbns'";;
  esac
}

# old versions left after a write run
#   fb: gstat -r SBTEST1 "total versions" / "max versions"
#   my: history list length
#   pg: n_dead_tup of sbtest1
gc_after() {
  case $1 in
    fb) $GSTAT -r -t SBTEST1 -user SYSDBA -password "$FBPASS" $FBFS 2>/dev/null \
          | grep -m1 -o 'total versions: [0-9]*, max versions: [0-9]*' | tr -d ',' | awk '{print "versions=" $3 " max_versions=" $6}';;
    my) echo "history_len=$(gc_sample my)";;
    pg) sleep 1; echo "dead_tup=$(psql_ -d sbns -c "select n_dead_tup from pg_stat_user_tables where relname = 'sbtest1'")";;
  esac
}

other_load() {
  local p e
  for p in $(pgrep '^sysbench'); do e=$(readlink /proc/$p/exe 2>/dev/null); [ "$e" = "$SB" ] || echo "sysbench($p) $e"; done
  pgrep -a '^(make|cc1|cc1plus|ld|gcc)$'
}

=== script main.sh
#!/bin/bash
# Main run: 2 passes x (Firebird client + server, MariaDB, PostgreSQL) x 7 tests x 1/4/16 threads,
# 30 s after 10 s warm-up. Golden copy before each write run, and once before the read tests.
# Durable settings are restored at the end (restore.sh).
set -u
. /root/nosync/lib.sh
R=$NS/runs.txt

# run_case <fb|my|pg> <label> <test> <threads> [extra args]
run_case() {
  local e=$1 label=$2 t=$3 th=$4; shift 4
  local f=$NS/raw/p${PASS}_${label}_${t}_${th}t.txt
  local write=1 args samples="" s pid rc o
  case $t in oltp_point_select|oltp_read_only) write=0;; esac
  case $e in fb) args=$FB_ARGS;; my) args=$MY_ARGS;; pg) args=$PG_ARGS;; esac
  o=$(other_load); [ -n "$o" ] && log "OTHER LOAD before $label $t ${th}t: $(echo "$o" | head -2 | tr '\n' ' ')"
  if [ $write = 1 ] || { [ $t = oltp_point_select ] && [ $th = 1 ] && [ $label != fbserver ]; }; then
    case $e in fb) fb_restore > /dev/null 2>&1;; my) my_restore;; pg) pg_restoredb;; esac \
      || { log "restore failed: $label $t ${th}t"; return 1; }
  fi
  pin_servers $e
  echo "# $(date '+%F %T') pass $PASS $label $t ${th}t, host loadavg $(cat /proc/loadavg)" > $f
  (cd $LUA && taskset -c $CLI_CPUS $SB $t.lua $args $DATA --threads=$th --time=$RUNTIME --warmup-time=$WARMUP "$@" run) >> $f 2>&1 &
  pid=$!
  if [ $write = 1 ]; then
    sleep $WARMUP
    while kill -0 $pid 2>/dev/null; do
      s=$(gc_sample $e); samples="$samples${samples:+,}${s:-?}"
      sleep 5
    done
  fi
  wait $pid; rc=$?
  local gc="" ; [ $write = 1 ] && gc=$(gc_after $e)
  echo "# gc samples (every 5 s of the measured part): $samples" >> $f
  echo "# after: $gc" >> $f
  local tps=$(sed -n 's/.*transactions: *[0-9]* *(\([0-9.]*\) per sec.*/\1/p' $f)
  local ign=$(sed -n 's/.*ignored errors: *\([0-9]*\).*/\1/p' $f)
  local fat=$(grep -c FATAL $f)
  local pw=$(sed -n 's/.*page writes \([0-9.]*\) .*/\1/p' $f)
  printf "%s | p%s %-8s %-22s %3s | rc %s tps %10s | ign %5s fatal %s | gc %s | %s | pw/stmt %s\n" \
    "$(date '+%T')" $PASS $label $t $th $rc "$tps" "$ign" "$fat" "$samples" "$gc" "$pw" | tee -a $R >> $NS/progress.log
}

engine_block() {
  local e=$1 t th
  only_engine $e
  [ $e = fb ] && { fb_restore > /dev/null 2>&1; }
  settings $e "pass$PASS-before"
  for t in $TESTS; do
    for th in $THREADS; do
      if [ $e = fb ]; then
        run_case fb fbclient $t $th
        run_case fb fbserver $t $th --firebird-autocommit=server
      else
        run_case $e $e $t $th
      fi
    done
    log "pass$PASS $e $t done"
  done
  settings $e "pass$PASS-after"
}

T0=$(date +%s)
log "main start"
# no fsync on commit: MariaDB and PostgreSQL configs (Firebird: alias sbwfs + FW off in fb_restore)
{ cat $NS/99-bench.cnf.durable; echo "innodb_flush_log_at_trx_commit = 2"; } > $MYCNF
{ cat $NS/bench.conf.durable; echo "synchronous_commit = off"; } > $PGCNF
for PASS in 1 2; do
  for e in fb my pg; do
    # restart after the MariaDB preload fix: pass 1 of Firebird is already done
    [ "$PASS:$e" = "1:fb" ] && [ -n "${SKIP_P1_FB:-}" ] && continue
    engine_block $e
  done
  log "pass$PASS done secs=$(( $(date +%s) - T0 ))"
done
log "main done secs=$(( $(date +%s) - T0 ))"
$NS/restore.sh
touch $NS/MAIN_DONE

=== script extra_my.sh
#!/bin/bash
# Extra (not in the task's configuration): MariaDB flush=2 with innodb_log_file_size = 1G
# (PostgreSQL has max_wal_size = 1GB). With the default 96M the checkpoint age stays at the
# limit and update runs stall. Write tests only, 2 passes. Then pass3.sh (it restores the durable settings).
set -u
. /root/nosync/lib.sh
. $NS/runcase.sh
R=$NS/runs.txt
log "extra my1g start"
{ cat $NS/99-bench.cnf.durable; echo "innodb_flush_log_at_trx_commit = 2"; echo "innodb_log_file_size = 1G"; } > $MYCNF
{ cat $NS/bench.conf.durable; echo "synchronous_commit = off"; } > $PGCNF
only_engine my
for PASS in 1 2; do
  settings my "my1g-pass$PASS-before"
  mariadb -N -e "select concat('innodb_log_file_size=', @@innodb_log_file_size div 1048576, 'M')" | tee -a $NS/settings.txt
  for t in oltp_update_non_index oltp_insert oltp_update_index oltp_read_write oltp_delete; do
    for th in $THREADS; do run_case my my1g $t $th; done
  done
  log "my1g pass$PASS done"
done
settings my "my1g-after"
touch $NS/EXTRA_DONE
$NS/pass3.sh

=== script restore.sh
#!/bin/bash
# Put the durable settings back (as recorded by setup.sh) and check them.
# State before: firebird + mariadb running, postgresql stopped.
set -u
. /root/nosync/lib.sh
log "restore start"
cp $NS/99-bench.cnf.durable $MYCNF
cp $NS/bench.conf.durable $PGCNF
cmp -s $NS/firebird.conf.durable /opt/firebird/firebird.conf || cp $NS/firebird.conf.durable /opt/firebird/firebird.conf
only_engine pg; settings pg restored
only_engine my; settings my restored
only_engine fb; settings fb restored
systemctl start mariadb
{
  echo "== services"; for x in fb my pg; do echo "$(svc $x) $(systemctl is-active $(svc $x))"; done
  echo "== $MYCNF"; cat $MYCNF
  echo "== $PGCNF"; cat $PGCNF
  echo "== firebird.conf (active lines)"; grep -v '^#' /opt/firebird/firebird.conf | grep -v '^\s*$'
  echo "== databases.conf sbw/sbwfs"; grep -A3 -E '^sbw' /opt/firebird/databases.conf
  for f in sbw sbwfs; do echo "== gstat -h $f"; $GSTAT -h /var/lib/fbdata/$f.fdb -user SYSDBA -password "$FBPASS" | grep -E 'Attributes|Next transaction'; done
} > $NS/durable_after.txt 2>&1
diff <(grep -v 'Next transaction' $NS/durable_before.txt) <(grep -v 'Next transaction' $NS/durable_after.txt) > $NS/durable_diff.txt
log "restore done, diff lines (except Next transaction): $(wc -l < $NS/durable_diff.txt)"
